- If White plays Re8+, your rook captures on e8 (`... Rxe8`), eliminating the mating threat.
- If White decides to trade rooks with Rxb8+, White gives up control of the 7th rank, opening a flight square for your king on h7 (`... Kh7`). After which, you survive the mating net and consolidate your position.
